# X59 — final read-only donor-bytes SEAL audit (2026-11-26)

## 0. Verdict

**OVERALL: PASS — no raw donor binary was copied into the repo today.** [O/D]
At the final census (2026-11-26 10:39:00.378598 local) every one of **5,427** new/untracked files in the
four audited roots (365,549,590 B), all 20 other untracked files and all 11
modified tracked files were hashed against a complete SHA-256 index of
**17,237 donor files (10,404,789,374 B, 14,546 unique hashes)** under
`C:\Games\Cossacks 3`. **Zero identical hashes.** The four large legacy derived
files were hashed separately: also zero matches. No new file with a donor-binary
extension (`.tga/.dds/.osm/.oss/.pattern/.map/.exe/.dll/.cdx/.ogg/.bmp/.aaf/
.aix/.mat/.prop/.actor/.lng`) exists in the scoped set; the 4,469 new `.png`
files are converted/derived and hash-clean.

|---|---|---|---|

| 2 | Hash >= 20 representative files; compare against donor files of matching name/extension; any identical hash = violation | **PASS** | 27-file name/extension/stem/size sample (§3, all donor candidate match counts 0) plus exhaustive whole-install hash comparison of all 5,427 files (§2). Second instrument (`Get-FileHash`) + Python repeat agreed 8/8 (§4). No violation was found, so none was declared. |
| 3 | Large derived atlas / winter atlas / split parts NOT committed; check `.gitignore` + `git ls-files` | **FAIL** (commit-policy check only, **not** a donor-copy violation) | Four legacy files are **TRACKED** despite ignore rules: `variant3d/assets/terrain/atlas/tile_index_atlas.png`, `.../tile_index_atlas_winter.png`, `variant3d/assets/terrain/atlas_parts/tile_index_atlas.part000`, `...part001` (§6). New `terrain_new` atlases are untracked and small (4,211,707 B summer ignored, 2,738,328 B winter currently **not** ignored). |

**Bottom line for COORD:** the donor-bytes SEAL is clean in every filesystem
snapshot taken today. The only FAIL is the *pre-existing* large-file tracking
policy, not anything written by this wave. No gate, integration or parity status
is changed by this report.

## 1. Multi-pass method and concurrency

**[O]** The swarm wrote files continuously during this audit, exactly as V49 §0
observed. Three independent hash snapshots were taken; all three are clean:

| Snapshot | Scoped new/untracked files | Bytes | Donor hash matches |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| A — first census | 906 | 246,036,436 | 0 |
| B — full donor-index pass (rebuilt after new files appeared) | 5,406 | 358,693,600 | 0 |
| C — final census, 2026-11-26 10:39:00.378598 | 5,427 | 365,549,590 | 0 |

**[D]** This is concurrent-writer growth (masks, lib index, atlas and new QA
reports appeared mid-audit), not a hash contradiction. **Snapshot C is the
authoritative inventory** (Appendix A). No file was edited by X59.

- **[O]** Repository reads were non-mutating: `git status --porcelain=v1 -z
  --untracked-files=all`, `git ls-files`, `git check-ignore -v [--no-index]`,
  `git log -1`, `git diff --exit-code -- app/` (exit 0), direct reads and
  streaming SHA-256. No `git add`, `git rm`, checkout, Godot job, gate or capture.
- **[O]** Donor reads were non-mutating: `os.walk` + `os.path.getsize` +
  SHA-256 over `C:\Games\Cossacks 3`. No donor executable was run.
- **[O]** Scratch files stayed only in
  `C:\Users\Maxym.Oboyshev\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\seal\`
  (`x59_new_files.txt`, `x59_sample_results.json`, `x59_full_crosscheck.json`,
  `x59_donor_hash_index.json`, `x59_final_snapshot.json`,
  `x59_final_second_instrument.json`, logs and audit scripts).
- **[O] Multiple-pass rule (CHARTER.md:88-93).** No hash match occurred, so no
  violation was declared. The protocol that would have been used on a match:
  re-hash both sides with `Get-FileHash`, repeat with Python `hashlib`, re-read
  both paths, and only then call a violation. Negative results are corroborated
  by two different hash implementations and by three time-separated censuses.
